# ConnectorR78 — sibling overlap, frozen R78-C1/C2

## Axes and scope

- **Coverage:** the frozen partial grader-class/member accounting surface; membership accounting is not additional task execution.
- **Calibration:** own-stamped-revision receipt location; location is not attestation, reproducibility, freshness, or authorship.
- Read the entire frozen `plans/round-78-claims.md` before commands. No predicate, target, control, or kill rule is changed here. This is a read-only ownership scan and one bounded report-out proposal, not admission or a counter verdict.

## Observed revisions and commands

All 20 resolving sibling directories were examined, including hidden committed wave scripts and archived fixtures. Each snapshot used a fixed full commit ID. `master` was resolved where present; the seven siblings without a master ref used HEAD. These pins are the observed branch tips at sampling time, not a claim that moving branches remain unchanged. No upstream/tracking ref was used.

Commands actually exercised through read-only subprocess calls (expanded for each lane/pin):

```text
git -C /home/vgpnk/Projects/shokunin/kengaku-shokunin rev-parse master
git -C /home/vgpnk/Projects/shokunin/<lane> rev-parse master
git -C /home/vgpnk/Projects/shokunin/<lane> rev-parse HEAD  # fallback and comparison
git -C /home/vgpnk/Projects/shokunin/<lane> ls-tree -r --name-only <PIN>
git -C /home/vgpnk/Projects/shokunin/<lane> show <PIN>:<tracked-path>
```

Tracked .py/.sh files were materialized under this seat's scratch only, then searched using the specialized `grep` tool, not executing the sibling instruments. JavaScript (.mjs/.js) was additionally scanned for B/C and targeted semantic provenance checks; otherwise kumitate/tabi would falsely appear to have no relevant implementation. SKILL.md/PROVINCE.md and index/ledger documents were also sampled for ownership context.

The exact search calls used `gitignore=false`, the scratch lane root plus `/**/*.py;.../**/*.sh` (and .mjs/.js for B/C), and these patterns; pagination continued with `skip=20,40,...` through the reported total:

```text
A: LANE_TIP|TOOL_SHA|stamp
B: (?i)(member.{0,40}ledger|ledger.{0,40}member|class.{0,40}ledger|ledger.{0,40}class|population.{0,40}ledger|ledger.{0,40}population|benchmark.{0,40}coverage)
C: (?i)(evidence.index|index.parity|parity.{0,25}index|receipt.{0,25}parity|parity.{0,25}receipt)
```

A is the requested `grep -rl 'LANE_TIP\|TOOL_SHA\|stamp' --include='*.py' --include='*.sh'` discovery, performed with the specialized search tool and line citations. B/C are broad candidate searches, not semantic proof that every lexical hit is a gate. All matching files receive a rule in the appendix. Each coordinate there is an observed matching line; several matches in one file are one candidate surface, not independent implementations.

## Contradictions and second-order effects

1. **Location versus reproduction:** kumitate receipt-evidence explicitly re-runs recorded argv in the judged revision (lines 15–19); R78-C2 checks self-location and tool identity, not this property. Reporting C2 as generic “verified/reproducible evidence” would overclaim calibration. Importing kumitate replay would also introduce execution/sandbox requirements outside the frozen contract.
2. **Ledger versus execution:** kagi member digests and mado exact-one-row tool inventory can be complete without executing a benchmark task. Kotoba removal detectors establish absent loader anchors, not task incapability. None can stand in for C1 executed receipt bytes or task-specific frozen reason detectors. Keep distinct members distinct from member-class pairs.
3. **Index parity versus location:** chousa round25-measure.sh:9-10 says exit 0 means measurements completed, not admission; its parity result is `EVIDENCE_MISFILED_ROWS` at lines 50–51. It is a counting probe, not a standalone zero-defect exit gate. A correctly indexed receipt can still be unlocated; a located receipt can still be misfiled. No scalar score merges these properties.
4. **Substrate versus domain:** generic receipt attestation belongs to kumitate; carrier sequencing belongs to kotodama; host loader removal belongs to kotoba; grader-member identity remains kengaku. Routing these surfaces avoids a second generic runtime contract or a falsely widened grader universe.
5. **Verification surface:** a TOOL_SHA spelling alone is not a located-evidence gate. Fixture timestamps, control generators, embedded source snapshots, and archived copies are retained in the appendix as unrelated candidates rather than inflated into duplicate implementations.

## One bounded proposed move

**Report out a two-level provenance boundary, without adding a gate:** phrase this round as “grader member accounting plus receipt self-location”; pair that statement with the pinned kumitate `receipt-evidence` and kotodama `check-receipt-seq` pointers as the owners of execution attestation and receipt sequencing. Explicitly state “no reproduction, freshness, authorship, or index-parity claim.” This connects the two R78 surfaces without coupling their counters or expanding either frozen universe.

- Affected axes: calibration improves by preventing stronger-than-measured claims; coverage is held because the full frozen grader population and its independent receipt/reason requirements remain unchanged. This is a proposed reporting improvement, not an observed counter gain.
- Runtime effect: none; no tool import, replay, new dependency, or cross-lane execution.
- Contract effect: preserve both frozen predicates; only state what each proves and where stronger mechanisms are owned.
- Substrate effect: retain owner-specific schemas and immutable pins instead of inventing a shared receipt format.
- Risks: readers may confuse the linked stronger gate with an exercised R78 check, or treat a moving sibling path as a stable pin. Label links “ownership reference, not run in R78” and retain the exact revisions above.
- Verification for Main: ensure the round report does not claim reproduction/freshness/index parity, and that each referenced sibling blob is resolved with `git -C <lane> show <PIN>:<path>`. Run the frozen C1/C2 checks only under Main's assignment; this connector ran neither counter nor sibling gate.

## Report-out list (do not absorb)

1. Kengaku owns only its benchmark grader-member ledger and the round77/78 receipt-location gate; ledgered unexecuted members remain unexecuted.
2. Route generic execution-receipt attestation/reproduction/freshness to **kumitate L1**, and lands/carrier topology to **kotodama L1**.
3. Route source receipt-index heading parity to **chousa L1**; distinguish its counter output from an exit-status gate. Route evidence citation resolution/round orchestration accounting to **tabi L1**.
4. Route host loader class-removal accounting to **kotoba L1**, source/citation populations to **kagi/kake/kama/kasegi L1** as itemized, tool inventories to **mado L1**, settlement to **kaidoku L1**, cleanup census to **souji L1**, receipt edit windows to **tameshi L1**, shipped-artifact receipts to **revenger L1**, and audio fixture member coverage to **audio L1**.
5. No exact duplicate of the two frozen kengaku-owned contracts was demonstrated by this scan. Do not count lexical stamp hits, historical mirrors, or the presence of a digest alone as a duplicate or an exercised verification.

CONNECTOR: 20 lanes, 0 duplicates, 22 adjacent

## Full candidate appendix

Every candidate file returned by A/B/C is below, including hidden historical scripts. A/B/C columns identify the command pattern and its first observed file:line hit. The 22 adjacent counts above are owner/surface families; 41 raw candidate files map to those families, with additional JavaScript semantic references explicitly identified in the main matrix.

Why codes (one sentence applies to every row bearing that code):

- **H:** Historical seat/attack/control/spec script or frozen fixture mirror is evidence data, not an independently owned production implementation of either R78 surface; do not absorb or execute it.
- **L:** The observed token occurs in lane-specific implementation/fixture content outside the frozen grader and receipt populations, with no demonstrated replacement of either R78 contract; do not infer a gate from a lexical match.
- **G2/G3:** Game measurement/fixture stamping is not receipt own-revision tool location, and no class/member-ledger or evidence-index parity candidate was found in that lane.
- **M:** Host/source observation timestamps and redaction/window handling do not certify verdict-tool identity at the receipt stamp.
- **T:** Host maintenance/package/kernel timing evidence is outside both R78 populations.
- A named **adjacent family** uses the exact why/owner sentence in the main matrix; archived copies remain H rather than independent adjacent owners.
